- [p] "Why choose us?!" — _Bellota 16px/normal w700 #4A4A4A start_
- [p] "The founders of The Shala Bali have a combined experience of over 70 years in the wellness industry. World renowned Yoga" — _Bellota 16px/normal w400 #4A4A4A start_
- [p] "Why Ubud?" — _Bellota 16px/normal w700 #4A4A4A start_
- [p] "Ubud is a hub for culture, arts and wellness. It has the widest range of body treatments and diversity of food, includin" — _Bellota 16px/normal w400 #4A4A4A start_
- [p] "Where is The Shala Bali?" — _Bellota 16px/normal w700 #4A4A4A start_
- [p] "We are situated a straight forward 1.5 hour drive from the international airport of Bali and a 30 minute drive from the" — _Bellota 16px/normal w400 #4A4A4A start_
- [p] "What can I find near by to The Shala Bali?" — _Bellota 16px/normal w700 #4A4A4A start_
- [p] "Right on our door step we have a number of well known restaurants and massage treatments. More shops, cafes, restaurants" — _Bellota 16px/normal w400 #4A4A4A start_
- [p] "What food is served at The Shala Bali restaurant?" — _Bellota 16px/normal w700 #4A4A4A start_
- [p] "We have a changing menu of delicious vegetarian and vegan dishes from around the world and traditional Balinese dishes." — _Bellota 16px/normal w400 #4A4A4A start_
- [p] "Why vegetarian?" — _Bellota 16px/normal w700 #4A4A4A start_
- [p] "What guests choose to include in their regular diets bears no judgment from us, however we believe plant based diets ben" — _Bellota 16px/normal w400 #4A4A4A start_
- [p] "Getting around Ubud?" — _Bellota 16px/normal w700 #4A4A4A start_
- [p] "Taxis are very readily available, private cars can be easily organized and scooters can be hired for a small cost. To hi" — _Bellota 16px/normal w400 #4A4A4A start_
- [p] "How do I get there from the Airport?" — _Bellota 16px/normal w700 #4A4A4A start_
- [p] "Taxis are available from the airport and are safe and reasonably priced. The Shala Bali can also arrange a private pick" — _Bellota 16px/normal w400 #4A4A4A start_
- [p] "Which airport do I fly into?" — _Bellota 16px/normal w700 #4A4A4A start_
- [p] "Denpasar airport is the only international airport in Bali. It is called Ngura Rai International Airport." — _Bellota 16px/normal w400 #4A4A4A start_
- [p] "What about visas?" — _Bellota 16px/normal w700 #4A4A4A start_
- [p] "Visitors from most countries can obtain a Visa On Arrival in Bali. This visa is valid for 30 days. Please check the spec" — _Bellota 16px/normal w400 #4A4A4A start_
- [p] "Taxes to enter and leave Bali?" — _Bellota 16px/normal w700 #4A4A4A start_
- [p] "There are no longer taxes to enter or leave Bali for mosy countries." — _Bellota 16px/normal w400 #4A4A4A start_
- [p] "Do I have to bring over USD or local currency?" — _Bellota 16px/normal w700 #4A4A4A start_
- [p] "It is not necessary to bring over large amounts of cash. There are many ATM machines for withdrawing money and exchange" — _Bellota 16px/normal w400 #4A4A4A start_
- [p] "Bali belly?" — _Bellota 16px/normal w700 #4A4A4A start_
- [p] "It is widely known that travelers can experience an upset stomach when visiting developing countries such as Bali. Norma" — _Bellota 16px/normal w400 #4A4A4A start_
- [p] "Vaccinations?" — _Bellota 16px/normal w700 #4A4A4A start_
- [p] "In general we do not consider vaccinations necessary. Although it is gradually being eradicated, a number of monkeys and" — _Bellota 16px/normal w400 #4A4A4A start_
- [p] "Safety?" — _Bellota 16px/normal w700 #4A4A4A start_
- [p] "As in any foreign country it is advised to maintain a higher level of vigilance. Drive by bag snatching does occur to bo" — _Bellota 16px/normal w400 #4A4A4A start_
